Q: What type of instrument is a hurdy gurdy
A: "A hurdy-gurdy is a stringed instrument that produces sound by a hand crank-turned, rosined wheel rubbing against the strings. It functions similarly to a violin bow, and melodies are played on a keyboard that presses \"tangents\" against the strings to change their pitch. The instrument typically has multiple drone strings, which provide a constant pitch accompaniment."

It functions similarly to a violin bow, and melodies are played on a keyboard that presses \"tangents\" against the strings to change their pitch. The instrument typically has multiple drone strings, which provide a constant pitch accompaniment
